	public static class CommonEventHandler
	{
		public static readonly EventHandler<InvalidValueEnteredEventArgs> InvalidValueErrorMessageBox = delegate(object sender, InvalidValueEnteredEventArgs e)
		{
			switch (e.ErrorType)
			{
			case InvalidValueErrorType.TypeError:
				MessageBox.Show(FormResource.message_invalidInput, FormResource.PSVSizing, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Hand);
				break;
			case InvalidValueErrorType.RangeError:
				MessageBox.Show(FormResource.msg_invalid, FormResource.PSVSizing, MessageBoxButton.OK, MessageBoxImage.Hand);
				break;
			case InvalidValueErrorType.OtherError:
				break;
			}
		};
	}

	public static class ResourceHelper
	{
		public static string GetResourceString(string str)
		{
			string text = FormResource.ResourceManager.GetString(str);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				text = ModelResource.ResourceManager.GetString(str);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return text;
			}
			return str;
		}

		public static string GetXmlFilePath(string filename)
		{
			return Path.Combine(Path.GetDirectoryName(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().Location), filename);
		}

		public static ImageSource ToImageSource(this Icon icon)
		{
			return Imaging.CreateBitmapSourceFromHIcon(icon.Handle, Int32Rect.Empty, BitmapSizeOptions.FromEmptyOptions());
		}
	}

	public static class ExtractDefaultPrefsFile
	{
		public static void Extract(string host)
		{
			string text = Path.Combine(Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.ApplicationData), "AspenTech");
			try
			{
				Directory.CreateDirectory(text);
				string path = Path.Combine(text, host + " Safety Defaults.SPRF");
				if (!File.Exists(path))
				{
					File.WriteAllBytes(path, AspenTech.EquipmentAnalysis.PSVControlResources.Properties.Resources.SafetyDefaults);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
	}
	public static class Format
	{
		public static bool CommaDecimalDelimiterUsed { get; set; }

		public static NumberFormatInfo CurrentFormatInfo => SystemCulture.NumberFormat;

		public static CultureInfo SystemCulture { get; set; }

		public static string FormatOrificeValue(object orgval)
		{
			if (!double.TryParse(orgval.ToString(), out var result))
			{
				return orgval.ToString();
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(result))
			{
				return FormResource.str_Empty;
			}
			double num = Math.Abs(result);
			if (num >= 10000.0)
			{
				return num.ToString("E3", CurrentFormatInfo);
			}
			return num.ToString("F3", CurrentFormatInfo);
		}

		public static string FormatNumber(object val, string emptyValue = null)
		{
			emptyValue = emptyValue ?? FormResource.str_Empty;
			if (val == null || val.ToString() == string.Empty)
			{
				return emptyValue;
			}
			if (!double.TryParse(val.ToString(), out var result))
			{
				return val.ToString();
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(result) || double.IsInfinity(result))
			{
				return emptyValue;
			}
			double num = Math.Abs(result);
			if (num.Equals(0.0))
			{
				return result.ToString("F4", CurrentFormatInfo);
			}
			if (num < 0.1)
			{
				return result.ToString("E3", CurrentFormatInfo);
			}
			if (num < 1.0 && result >= 0.1)
			{
				return Math.Round(result, 4).ToString("F4", CurrentFormatInfo);
			}
			if (num >= 10000.0)
			{
				return result.ToString("E3", CurrentFormatInfo);
			}
			int length = Math.Truncate(Math.Round(num, 5, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)).ToString().Length;
			if (length > 4)
			{
				return result.ToString("F4", CurrentFormatInfo);
			}
			int digits = 4 - length;
			if (num.ToString().Length <= 5)
			{
				return result.ToString($"F{digits.ToString()}", CurrentFormatInfo);
			}
			return Math.Round(result, digits).ToString($"F{digits.ToString()}", CurrentFormatInfo);
		}
	}

	public static class PipeSizeDataService
	{
		private static List<PipeSizeInfo> AllPipeSizes;

		static PipeSizeDataService()
		{
			AllPipeSizes = new List<PipeSizeInfo>();
			using StringReader textReader = new StringReader(AspenTech.EquipmentAnalysis.PSVControlResources.Properties.Resources.PipeSize);
			DataSet obj = (DataSet)new XmlSerializer(typeof(DataSet)).Deserialize(textReader);
			DataTable dataTable = obj.Tables[0];
			DataTable dataTable2 = obj.Tables[1];
			foreach (DataRow row in dataTable.Rows)
			{
				PipeSizeInfo pipeSizeInfo = new PipeSizeInfo();
				string[] array = row[0].ToString().Split(' ');
				pipeSizeInfo.NominalDiameter = array[0];
				pipeSizeInfo.Schedule = row[1].ToString();
				pipeSizeInfo.InternalDiameter = row[2].ToString();
				pipeSizeInfo.WallThickness = row[3].ToString();
				AllPipeSizes.Add(pipeSizeInfo);
			}
			foreach (DataRow row2 in dataTable2.Rows)
			{
				PipeSizeInfo pipeSizeInfo2 = new PipeSizeInfo();
				string[] array2 = row2[0].ToString().Split(' ');
				pipeSizeInfo2.NominalDiameter = array2[0];
				pipeSizeInfo2.Schedule = row2[1].ToString();
				pipeSizeInfo2.InternalDiameter = row2[2].ToString();
				pipeSizeInfo2.WallThickness = row2[3].ToString();
				AllPipeSizes.Add(pipeSizeInfo2);
			}
		}

		public static double GetInnerDiameter(string Schedule, string NominalDiameter)
		{
			PipeSizeInfo pipeSizeInfo = AllPipeSizes.FirstOrDefault((PipeSizeInfo p) => p.NominalDiameter == NominalDiameter && p.Schedule == Schedule);
			if (pipeSizeInfo == null)
			{
				return double.NaN;
			}
			if (double.TryParse(pipeSizeInfo.InternalDiameter, out var result))
			{
				return UnitConversion.ConvertToDefaultDisplayValue("mm", "in", result, "SmallLength");
			}
			return double.NaN;
		}

		public static void GetDiameterAndThickness(string Schedule, double NominalDiameter, out double ID, out double WallThickness)
		{
			ID = double.NaN;
			WallThickness = double.NaN;
			PipeSizeInfo pipeSizeInfo = AllPipeSizes.FirstOrDefault((PipeSizeInfo p) => p.NominalDiameter == NominalDiameter.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) && p.Schedule == Schedule);
			if (pipeSizeInfo != null)
			{
				if (double.TryParse(pipeSizeInfo.InternalDiameter, out var result))
				{
					ID = UnitConversion.ConvertToDefaultDisplayValue(UnitStrings.SmallLength_mm, UnitStrings.SmallLength_in, result, "SmallLength");
				}
				if (double.TryParse(pipeSizeInfo.WallThickness, out var result2))
				{
					WallThickness = UnitConversion.ConvertToDefaultDisplayValue(UnitStrings.SmallLength_mm, UnitStrings.SmallLength_in, result2, "SmallLength");
				}
			}
		}

		public static List<string> GetPipeScheduleList(double nd)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>
			{
				FormResource.str_Empty,
				FormResource.LineSizingSchedule_Actual
			};
			if (double.IsNaN(nd))
			{
				return list;
			}
			string[] array = (from pipeSize in AllPipeSizes
				let ndString = nd.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)
				where pipeSize.NominalDiameter == ndString
				select pipeSize.Schedule).ToArray();
			int[] array2 = (from key in array
				let index = SizeData.Schedules.IndexOf(key)
				where index >= 0
				select index).ToArray();
			if (array2.Length != array.Length)
			{
				return new List<string>();
			}
			Array.Sort(array2, array);
			list.AddRange(array);
			return list;
		}

		public static List<string> GetPipeSchedules()
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			list.Add(FormResource.str_Empty);
			list.Add(FormResource.LineSizingSchedule_Actual);
			list.AddRange(SizeData.Schedules);
			return list;
		}
	}
	public static class CheckAccessDriverBitness
	{
		private static bool hasAccessConnectivity = true;

		public static bool IsAccessBitLevelKnown { get; private set; } = false;

		private static void _RunAccessBitnessCheck()
		{
			string tempPath = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), Path.GetTempFileName());
			ExtractPipeScheduleFile(tempPath);
			string connectionString = AccessConnectivity.getAccessProviderString() + " Data Source = " + tempPath;
			string selectCommandText = "select Nominal,Schedule,Internal_Diameter,Wall_Thickness from Carbon";
			try
			{
				using (OleDbConnection oleDbConnection = new OleDbConnection(connectionString))
				{
					oleDbConnection.Open();
					using (OleDbDataAdapter oleDbDataAdapter = new OleDbDataAdapter(selectCommandText, oleDbConnection))
					{
						using DataSet dataSet = new DataSet();
						oleDbDataAdapter.Fill(dataSet, "Carbon");
					}
					oleDbConnection.Close();
				}
				hasAccessConnectivity = true;
				OleDbConnection.ReleaseObjectPool();
				GC.Collect();
				GC.WaitForPendingFinalizers();
				GC.Collect();
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				hasAccessConnectivity = false;
			}
			finally
			{
				IsAccessBitLevelKnown = true;
				Task.Run(() => TryDeleteFile(tempPath));
			}
		}

		private static bool TryDeleteFile(string filename)
		{
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			do
			{
				num++;
				try
				{
					File.Delete(filename);
					flag = true;
				}
				catch
				{
					if (num < 10)
					{
						Thread.Sleep(20 * num * num);
					}
				}
			}
			while (num < 10 && !flag);
			return flag;
		}

		private static void ExtractPipeScheduleFile(string filename)
		{
			using (MemoryStream stream = new MemoryStream(AspenTech.EquipmentAnalysis.PSVControlResources.Properties.Resources.pipe_schedule))
			{
				using GZipStream gZipStream = new GZipStream(stream, CompressionMode.Decompress);
				using FileStream fileStream = File.Create(filename);
				byte[] buffer = new byte[65536];
				int count;
				while ((count = gZipStream.Read(buffer, 0, 65536)) != 0)
				{
					fileStream.Write(buffer, 0, count);
				}
			}
			File.SetAttributes(filename, FileAttributes.Temporary);
		}

		public static bool is64bitAccessDriverPresent()
		{
			if (!IsAccessBitLevelKnown)
			{
				_RunAccessBitnessCheck();
			}
			if (IntPtr.Size == 8 && hasAccessConnectivity)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool is32bitAccessDriverPresent()
		{
			if (!IsAccessBitLevelKnown)
			{
				_RunAccessBitnessCheck();
			}
			if (IntPtr.Size == 4 && hasAccessConnectivity)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public static class AccessConnectivity
	{
		public static string getAccessProviderString()
		{
			return "Provider = Microsoft.ACE.OLEDB.12.0 ;";
		}
	}

		public static List<string> Schedules { get; } = _schedules.ToList();

		public static IEnumerable<ComboBoxDataItem> NominalSizes { get; } = ((IEnumerable<double>)_nominalSizes).Select((Func<double, ComboBoxDataItem>)((double item) => new ComboBoxDataItem((object)item, Format.FormatNumber(item))));

		public static IEnumerable<ComboBoxDataItem> RuptureDiskSizes => _ruptureDiskSizes.Select((double value) => UnitConversion.ConvertToDefaultDisplayValue(UnitStrings.SmallLength_in, UnitConversion.GetDefaultUnit("SmallLength2"), value, "SmallLength2")).Select((Func<double, ComboBoxDataItem>)((double item) => new ComboBoxDataItem((object)item, Format.FormatNumber(item))));
	}

		private static Dictionary<string, object> _enumTranslatorStorage = new Dictionary<string, object>();

		private static EnumTranslator<T> GenerateEnumTranslator<T>(Func<string, string> resourceKeyFormatProvider) where T : struct
		{
			Type type = typeof(T);
			if (!type.IsEnum)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("The type of T must be an enum.");
			}
			Dictionary<T, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<T, string>();
			string[] names = Enum.GetNames(type);
			if (resourceKeyFormatProvider == null)
			{
				resourceKeyFormatProvider = (string enumName) => ResourceHelper.GetResourceString(type.Name + "_" + enumName);
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (object value in Enum.GetValues(type))
			{
				dictionary.Add((T)value, resourceKeyFormatProvider(names[num++]));
			}
			return new EnumTranslator<T>(dictionary, FormResource.str_Empty);
		}

		public static EnumTranslator<T> GetEnumTranslator<T>(Func<string, string> resourceKeyFormatProvider = null) where T : struct
		{
			string fullName = typeof(T).FullName;
			if (!_enumTranslatorStorage.ContainsKey(fullName))
			{
				_enumTranslatorStorage.Add(fullName, GenerateEnumTranslator<T>(resourceKeyFormatProvider));
			}
			return _enumTranslatorStorage[fullName] as EnumTranslator<T>;
		}

		public static EnumTranslator<T> GetEnumTranslator<T>(string resourcePrefix) where T : struct
		{
			return GetEnumTranslator<T>((string s) => ResourceHelper.GetResourceString(resourcePrefix + s));
		}
	}

	public static class UnitConversion
	{
		private static Dictionary<string, List<UnitStruct>> _unitsDictionary = null;

		private static readonly string _filepath = ResourceHelper.GetXmlFilePath("AspenTech.EquipmentAnalysis.Units.xml");

		private static XDocument _doc = null;

		private static Dictionary<string, List<UnitTypeStruct>> _typeDictionary = null;

		private static Dictionary<string, string> _typeCalUnitDic = null;

		private static Dictionary<string, List<UnitStruct>> _calunitsDictionary = null;

		private static object _unitsDictionaryLock = new object();

		public static List<string> GetUnits(string unitKey)
		{
			if (_unitsDictionary.ContainsKey(unitKey))
			{
				return _unitsDictionary[unitKey].Select((UnitStruct us) => us.name).ToList();
			}
			return new List<string>();
		}

		public static string GetCalculationUnit(string unitType)
		{
			if (_typeDictionary == null)
			{
				ParseUnitsFile();
			}
			if (_typeDictionary == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			switch (unitType)
			{
			case "GaugePressure":
				return UnitStrings.GaugePressure_barG;
			case "AbsolutePressure":
				return UnitStrings.AbsPressure_barA;
			case "DeltaPressure":
				return UnitStrings.DeltaPressure_bar;
			default:
			{
				if (!_typeDictionary.TryGetValue(unitType, out var value) || !value.Any())
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				return value.First().calcunit;
			}
			}
		}

		public static string GetDefaultUnit(string unittype)
		{
			if (_typeDictionary == null)
			{
				ParseUnitsFile();
			}
			if (_typeDictionary == null || !_typeDictionary.ContainsKey(unittype))
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return (from uts in _typeDictionary[unittype]
				where uts.us == Common.PSVUnitSet
				select uts.displayunit).FirstOrDefault();
		}

		public static string GetDefaultUnit(string unittype, Common.eUnitSet set)
		{
			if (_typeDictionary == null)
			{
				ParseUnitsFile();
			}
			if (_typeDictionary == null || !_typeDictionary.ContainsKey(unittype))
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return (from uts in _typeDictionary[unittype]
				where uts.us == set
				select uts.displayunit).FirstOrDefault();
		}

		public static void ParseUnitsFile()
		{
			lock (_unitsDictionaryLock)
			{
				if (!File.Exists(_filepath) || _unitsDictionary != null)
				{
					return;
				}
				_unitsDictionary = new Dictionary<string, List<UnitStruct>>();
				if (_calunitsDictionary == null)
				{
					_calunitsDictionary = new Dictionary<string, List<UnitStruct>>();
				}
				if (_typeCalUnitDic == null)
				{
					_typeCalUnitDic = new Dictionary<string, string>();
				}
				if (_typeDictionary == null)
				{
					_typeDictionary = new Dictionary<string, List<UnitTypeStruct>>();
				}
				_doc = XDocument.Load(_filepath);
				foreach (XElement item2 in _doc.Root.Elements())
				{
					string attributeValue = XmlHelper.GetAttributeValue(item2, "Name");
					string attributeValue2 = XmlHelper.GetAttributeValue(item2, "CalculationUnit");
					List<UnitTypeStruct> list = new List<UnitTypeStruct>();
					foreach (XElement targetElement in XmlHelper.GetTargetElements(item2, "UnitSystem"))
					{
						list.Add(new UnitTypeStruct
						{
							us = (Common.eUnitSet)Enum.Parse(typeof(Common.eUnitSet), XmlHelper.GetAttributeValue(targetElement, "Name")),
							displayunit = XmlHelper.GetAttributeValue(targetElement, "DisplayUnit"),
							calcunit = attributeValue2
						});
					}
					_typeDictionary.Add(attributeValue, list);
					_typeCalUnitDic.Add(attributeValue, attributeValue2);
					List<UnitStruct> list2 = new List<UnitStruct>();
					List<UnitStruct> list3 = new List<UnitStruct>();
					foreach (XElement targetElement2 in XmlHelper.GetTargetElements(item2, "Unit"))
					{
						UnitStruct item = new UnitStruct
						{
							name = XmlHelper.GetAttributeValue(targetElement2, "Name")
						};
						string elementValue = XmlHelper.GetElementValue(targetElement2, "Factor");
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(elementValue))
						{
							item.factor = double.Parse(elementValue, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						}
						string elementValue2 = XmlHelper.GetElementValue(targetElement2, "OffSet");
						if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(elementValue2))
						{
							item.offset = double.Parse(elementValue2, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
						}
						list3.Add(item);
						if (targetElement2.HasAttributes && XmlHelper.GetAttributeValue(targetElement2, "Exclude") == null)
						{
							list2.Add(item);
						}
					}
					_unitsDictionary.Add(attributeValue, list2);
					_calunitsDictionary.Add(attributeValue, list3);
				}
			}
		}

		public static double ConvertToDefaultDisplayValue(string selectedUnit, string displayUnit, double inputVal, string unittype)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(inputVal))
			{
				return inputVal;
			}
			if (string.Compare(displayUnit, selectedUnit, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return inputVal;
			}
			string text = CalculationUnit(unittype);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				return inputVal;
			}
			if (string.Compare(text, selectedUnit, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return ConvertInternalToDisplayValue(displayUnit, inputVal, unittype);
			}
			try
			{
				double inputVal2 = ConvertDisplayToInternalValue(selectedUnit, inputVal, unittype);
				double num = ConvertInternalToDisplayValue(displayUnit, inputVal2, unittype);
				switch (unittype)
				{
				case "AbsolutePressure":
				case "GaugePressure":
				case "DeltaPressure":
				case "TankPressure1":
				case "TankPressure2":
					if (inputVal == 0.0 && Common.IsSameValue(Math.Round(num, 3, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero), inputVal))
					{
						num = inputVal;
					}
					break;
				}
				return num;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return inputVal;
			}
		}

		private static double ConvertDisplayToInternalValue(string displayUnit, double inputVal, string unittype)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!_calunitsDictionary.ContainsKey(unittype))
				{
					return inputVal;
				}
				UnitStruct unitStruct = _calunitsDictionary[unittype].FirstOrDefault((UnitStruct p) => string.Compare(p.name, displayUnit, ignoreCase: true) == 0);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitStruct.name))
				{
					return inputVal;
				}
				double offset = unitStruct.offset;
				double factor = unitStruct.factor;
				return (inputVal - offset) / factor;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return inputVal;
			}
		}

		private static double ConvertInternalToDisplayValue(string displayUnit, double inputVal, string unittype)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!_calunitsDictionary.ContainsKey(unittype))
				{
					return inputVal;
				}
				UnitStruct unitStruct = _calunitsDictionary[unittype].FirstOrDefault((UnitStruct p) => string.Compare(p.name, displayUnit, ignoreCase: true) == 0);
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitStruct.name))
				{
					return inputVal;
				}
				double offset = unitStruct.offset;
				double factor = unitStruct.factor;
				return inputVal * factor + offset;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return inputVal;
			}
		}

		private static string CalculationUnit(string unittype)
		{
			if (_typeCalUnitDic.ContainsKey(unittype))
			{
				return _typeCalUnitDic[unittype];
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}
	}
	internal static class XmlHelper
	{
		public const string name = "Name";

		public const string factor = "Factor";

		public const string offset = "OffSet";

		public const string calunit = "CalculationUnit";

		public const string disunit = "DisplayUnit";

		public const string usys = "UnitSystem";

		public static string GetAttributeValue(XElement node, string aname)
		{
			if (node.Attribute(aname) == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return node.Attribute(aname).Value;
		}

		public static List<XElement> GetTargetElements(XElement parent, string ename)
		{
			return parent.Elements(ename).ToList();
		}

		public static string GetElementValue(XElement root, string ename)
		{
			XElement xElement = root.Elements(ename).FirstOrDefault();
			if (xElement != null)
			{
				return xElement.Value;
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		public static XElement GetTargetElement(XElement parent, string path)
		{
			return parent.XPathSelectElement(path);
		}

		public static XElement GetTargetUnitNode(XElement parent, string unitname)
		{
			IEnumerable<XElement> source = parent.XPathSelectElements("./UnitType/Unit");
			if (source.Count() > 0)
			{
				return source.FirstOrDefault((XElement p) => string.Compare(unitname, GetAttributeValue(p, "Name"), ignoreCase: true) == 0);
			}
			return null;
		}
	}

	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return "-";
			}
			if (value.ToString() == double.NaN.ToString() || value.ToString() == string.Empty)
			{
				return FormResource.str_Empty;
			}
			return value;
		}

		{
			if (value.ToString() == FormResource.str_Empty)
			{
				return null;

			return value;
		}
	}

	public class EditorVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value.GetType() != typeof(EditorType))
			{
				return Binding.DoNothing;
			}
			if (parameter.GetType() != typeof(EditorType))
			{
				return Binding.DoNothing;
			}
			EditorType num = (EditorType)value;
			EditorType editorType = (EditorType)parameter;
			return (num != editorType) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return Binding.DoNothing;
		}

	[ValueConversion(typeof(bool), typeof(System.Windows.Media.Brush))]
	public class EditorForegroundConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value.GetType() != typeof(bool))
			{
				return Binding.DoNothing;
			}
			bool result = false;
			bool.TryParse(parameter.ToString(), out result);
			bool num = (bool)value;
			System.Windows.Media.Brush gray = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Gray;
			System.Windows.Media.Brush black = System.Windows.Media.Brushes.Black;
			if (num != result)
			{
				return black;
			}
			return gray;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

	public class CalculatedOrificeToSelectButtonIsEnabledConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return DependencyProperty.UnsetValue;
			}
			string text = System.Convert.ToString(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text) || text.ToLower() == "nan")
			{
				return false;
			}
			double result = 0.0;
			double.TryParse(text, out result);
			return result != -1.0;
		}

		}

	public class CalculatedRDDiameterToSelectButtonIsEnabledConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values != null && values.Length == 2)
			{
				object obj = values[0];
				object obj2 = values[1];
				if (obj2 != null && obj2.GetType() == typeof(int))
				{
					if ((int)obj2 == 7)
					{
						return false;
					}
					if (obj != null && !string.IsNullOrEmpty(obj.ToString()) && !string.Equals(obj.ToString(), "NaN") && double.TryParse(obj.ToString(), out var _))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)

	public class CalculatedOrByUserConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return Binding.DoNothing;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(value.ToString()))
			{
				return Binding.DoNothing;
			}
			return value.ToString() == "Calculated";
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if ((bool)value)
			{
				return "Calculated";
			}
			return "by User";
		}
	}

	[ValueConversion(typeof(int), typeof(Visibility))]
	public class IntToVisConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			int num = (int)value;
			switch (parameter.ToString())
			{
			case "rupturedisk":
				if (num == 0)
				{
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			case "psv":
				if (num == 1)
				{
					return Visibility.Collapsed;
				}
				return Visibility.Visible;
			case "prdsuffix":
				if (num > 1)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			case "ksh":
				if (num == 2)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			case "noise":
				if (num == 0 || num == 2)
				{
					return Visibility.Visible;
				}
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			default:
				return Binding.DoNothing;
			}
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	public class BuiltUpBackPressureSourceConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			switch (parameter.ToString())
			{
			case "fixed":
				if ((BuiltUpBackpressureSource)Enum.Parse(typeof(BuiltUpBackpressureSource), value.ToString()) == BuiltUpBackpressureSource.PctOfSet)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				break;
			case "manual":
				if ((BuiltUpBackpressureSource)Enum.Parse(typeof(BuiltUpBackpressureSource), value.ToString()) == BuiltUpBackpressureSource.Fixed)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				break;
			case "rated":
				if ((LineRatingFlowSpec)Enum.Parse(typeof(LineRatingFlowSpec), value.ToString()) == LineRatingFlowSpec.RatedArea)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				break;
			case "required":
				if ((LineRatingFlowSpec)Enum.Parse(typeof(LineRatingFlowSpec), value.ToString()) == LineRatingFlowSpec.Required)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				break;
			}
			return flag;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			string text = parameter.ToString();
			bool flag = (bool)value;
			object result = Binding.DoNothing;
			switch (text)
			{
			case "fixed":
				if (flag)
				{
					result = BuiltUpBackpressureSource.PctOfSet;
				}
				break;
			case "manual":
				if (flag)
				{
					result = BuiltUpBackpressureSource.Fixed;
				}
				break;
			case "rated":
				if (flag)
				{
					result = LineRatingFlowSpec.RatedArea;
				}
				break;
			case "required":
				if (flag)
				{
					result = LineRatingFlowSpec.Required;
				}
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(SpecifierType), typeof(System.Windows.FontStyle))]
	public class SpecifierTypeFontStyleConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			switch (value as SpecifierType?)
			{
			case null:
			case SpecifierType.User:
			case SpecifierType.Calculation:
			case SpecifierType.Unspecified:
				return FontStyles.Normal;
			case SpecifierType.Default:
				return FontStyles.Italic;
			default:
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", "Internal error: Invalid specifier type or argument wasn't a specifier type");
			}
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	[ValueConversion(typeof(UnitOfMeasure), typeof(string))]
	public class UnitOfMeasureStringConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public static readonly Dictionary<string, UnitOfMeasure> UnitsBySymbol = Units.List.ToDictionary((UnitOfMeasure uom) => uom.Symbol, (UnitOfMeasure uom) => uom);

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (!(value is UnitOfMeasure unitOfMeasure))
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return unitOfMeasure.Symbol;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			string key = ((value is string text) ? text : string.Empty);
			if (UnitsBySymbol.TryGetValue(key, out var value2))
			{
				return value2;
			}
			return Units.One;
		}
	}
	[ValueConversion(typeof(UnitOfMeasure), typeof(string))]
	public class PressureUnitOfMeasureStringConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public static readonly Dictionary<string, UnitOfMeasure> UnitsBySymbol = UnitOfMeasureStringConverter.UnitsBySymbol.Where((KeyValuePair<string, UnitOfMeasure> p) => p.Value.UnitType == UnitTypes.Pressure).ToDictionary((KeyValuePair<string, UnitOfMeasure> p) => p.Key, (KeyValuePair<string, UnitOfMeasure> p) => p.Value);

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (parameter is string)
			{
				text = (string)parameter;
			}
			else if (parameter is PressureReference pressureReference)
			{
				text = pressureReference.ToString().First().ToString();
			}
			if (!(value is UnitOfMeasure unitOfMeasure))
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return unitOfMeasure.Symbol + text;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			string text = value as string;
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Cannot convert null or empty string to Pressure Unit");
			}
			UnitOfMeasure value2 = null;
			if (UnitsBySymbol.TryGetValue(text.Substring(0, text.Length - 1), out value2))
			{
				return value2;
			}
			if (UnitsBySymbol.TryGetValue(text, out value2))
			{
				return value2;
			}
			throw new ArgumentException($"Cannot convert {text} to PressureUnit");
		}
	}

	public class FormatNumberConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return Format.FormatNumber(value);
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (double.TryParse(value.ToString(), NumberStyles.Any, culture, out var result))
			{
				return result;
			}
			return value.ToString();
		}
	}
	public class ReadOnlyNumericalFieldConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value == null || string.IsNullOrEmpty(value.ToString()))
			{
				return FormResource.str_Empty;
			}
			string text = Format.FormatNumber(value);
			ValueEditor val = (ValueEditor)((parameter is ValueEditor) ? parameter : null);
			if (val != null && ((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Tag != null)
			{
				string defaultUnit = UnitConversion.GetDefaultUnit(((FrameworkElement)(object)val).Tag.ToString());
				return $"{text} {defaultUnit}";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class NotConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return !(bool)value;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return !(bool)value;
		}
	}
	public class RLSDataSourceToFormatStringConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return "-";
			}
			return Format.FormatNumber(value);
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (double.TryParse(value.ToString().Replace(",", "."), NumberStyles.Any, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result))
			{
				return result;
			}
			return value.ToString();
		}
	}
